piṟaṉmaṉai nōkkāta pērāṇmai cāṉṟōrk
kaṟaṉoṉṟō vāṉṟa voḻukku.
nalakkuriyār yāreṉiṉ nāmanīr vaippil
piṟarkkuriyāḷ tōḷtōyā tār.
aṟaṉvaraiyāṉ alla ceyiṉum piṟaṉvaraiyāḷ
peṇmai nayavāmai naṉṟu.
ik kuṟaṭpākkaḷil yāṇṭāyiṉum āṇmakaṉ piṟa mātarai viḻaivatu kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatā eṉpatai aṉparkaḷ uṟṟu nōkkuvārkaḷāka. ivvatikāram, oḻukkamuṭaimai eṉṉum atikārattait toṭarntu niṟṟaluṅ karutaṟpālatu. oḻukkat tuṟaikaḷuḷ talaiyāyatu - uyir pōṉṟatu- piṟaṉil viḻaiyāmai eṉpataiyaṉṟō attoṭarpu aṟivuṟuttukiṟatu?

cilar purāṇak kataikaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvar. ivar oḻukkattil nāṭṭamuṭaiyavarākār. cila tattuva nuṭpaṅkaḷaip peṇṇāka uruvakap paṭuttik kavikaḷ pāṭiyataip poruḷāk koḷvatu maṭamai. avaṟṟaik kāṭṭi ulakai ēmāṟṟap pukuvatu kayamai. oruvaṉ oruttiyuṭaṉ vāḻtalē aṟam; aṟam. pala peṇkaḷai oruvaṉ maṇakkalām eṉṉuṅ kaṭṭukkataikaḷ iṉi otukkappaṭal vēṇṭum.
vipacārak kūṭṭattai oḻuṅkupaṭuttal
piṟappāl patiyilār eṉṟu collappaṭuṅ kūṭṭattiṉ kuṟaipāṭukaḷai niṟaivu ceytal aritaṉṟu. ulakil potuvāka vuḷḷa patiyilā vipacārak kūṭṭattaic cīrceyvatu mika aritu. ikkūṭṭam perukutaṟkuk kāraṇam vaṟumaiyāyiruttalāl, aṟavōr āṅkāṅkē viṭutic cālaikaḷ amaittu, appeṇ maṇikaṭkuc cōṟu kūṟai utavi, kalvi kaṟpittu, kaittoḻil pōtittu, virumpuvōrkku maṇañ ceytu vaippatu nalam. nāṭṭil celvar palar illāmalillai. aṉṉār maṉam it tuṟaiyil tirumputal vēṇṭum. ēḻaip peṇmakkaṭkeṉa ōr aṟaccālai amaippatu ettuṇaiyō vēṟu pala aṟaccālai kaḷ niṟuviya payaṉai nalkum. celvar tamatu celvattai ivvaṟat tuṟaiyil payaṉpaṭuttuvārāka. aracu, celvattaip potumai yākkiṉ vaṟumaip pēyē māṇṭu pōkum.
celvarkaḷāl peṇṇulakaṅ keṭal
ulakil celvarē pala kēṭṭiṟkuṅ kāraṇarāyiruttal kaṇkūṭu. ēḻaittoḻilāḷar uḻaippāl peṟuñ celvattai mutalāḷikaḷ palatiṟak kaḷiyāṭalkaḷukkuc celavu ceytal peruntavaṟu. kaḷiyāṭalkaḷuḷ peṇmakkaḷaik kaṟpaḻikkum āṭal talaiyāyatu. celvarkkup peṇ vaṭivaṅkaḷ eṉṉa vākat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉavō teriyavillai. peṇ oru kaḷip poruḷ eṉpatu aṉṉār karuttup pōlum!
celvar poruṭṭup patiyilāp peṇmakkaḷ taṅkaḷ nilaiyai maṟantu, taṅkaḷai oru vilaip poruḷākak karuti vāḻkiṟārkaḷ. celvar koṭumaiyāl peṇmakkaḷiṉ tey vikamum karantu niṟkiṟatu. teyvap peṇkaḷaip pēyākkuñ celvar āṇṭavaṉai uṇarum nāḷum uṇṭō? peṇṇait teyvamākap pōṟṟum oru nilai peṟuvōṉ kaṭavuḷaik kāṇum aṉpaṉāvaṉ; aruḷāḷaṉāvaṉ.
ulakil patiyilār kūṭṭam eṉṟoṉṟu illātavāṟu oḻikka āṅkāṅkē aṉpum aṟivum vāynta periyōr muyalvārāka. avar muyaṟcikku iyaṟkaiyum iṟaiyum tuṇaiceyyum. iyaṟkai iṟaiyiṉ nōkkam peṇṇulakil patiyilār iruttal vēṇṭumeṉpataṉṟu. ceyaṟkai oḻiya, iyaṟkai iṟaiyum tuṇaipuriyum. ulakil patiyilāk kūṭṭam oḻika; oḻika. peṇṇulakil kaṟai aḻika; aḻika. peṇṇulaku ōṅka; ōṅka.
aṉpiṉ viḻaiyār poruḷviḻaiyum āytoṭiyār
iṉco liḻukkut tarum 	-tiruvaḷḷuvar


12. kaimmai
(kaimpeṇ nilaiyum ciṟumaikaḷum - kuḻantai maṇak koṭumai - aiṉa kaṇitak kuṟippu - intu matattiṉ poṟuppu - maṟumaṇam - cāttiram; karmam - kaimpeṇkaḷiṉ tuyar - kaḷaiyum vaḻi).
tirumaṇañ ceytu koṇṭa orutti, taṉ koḻunaṉaip paṟṟiya oru ceytiyum peṟātoḻiyiṉum allatu avaḷ talaivaṉ kolaiyuṇṭā ṉāyiṉum allatu iṟantu paṭṭāṉāyiṉum avaḷ kaṉṉiyā yiruppā ḷāyiṉ, avaḷukku maṟumaṇañ ceytu koḷḷum urimai uṇṭu. 	-pōtāyaṉar.
nāṭṭuk kaimpeṇ nilai
kaimmai eṉṉun talaippiṭumpōtē yāṉ piṟanta nāṭṭiṉmītu nāṭṭañ celkiṟatu. kaimmai nilai peṇṇula kiṟkum āṇulakiṟkum potuvāṉatu. annilai vāḻvil irupālāruḷ evarkkēṉum oruvarkku uṟāmaṟ pōkātu. ulakil pala iṭaṅkaḷil āṇkaimmaiyaraip pōlavē peṇkaimmaiyarum vāḻntu varukiṉṟaṉar. cila iṭaṅkaḷil ciṟappāka namatu nāṭṭil kaimmai eytum peṇmakkaṭkup palatiṟac ciṟumaikaḷ uṇṭu. namatu nāṭṭiṟ potuvākap peṇṇulakiṟkē ciṟumai atikam. atilum kaimpeṇkaḷuk kuḷḷa ciṟumaikaḷ collaṟpālaṉavalla. m¢áWikfŸ ÃidÉYW« nghJ v‹ò be¡F ÉL»wJ; cŸs§FiH »wJ ‘ï¥ghG« eh£oš V‹ ãwªnjh«? eṉṟu niṉaintu niṉaintu iraṅkutalum nērkiṟatu.
peṇmakaḷ kaimmai eytiṉāl eṉṉa? āṇ makaṉ annilai eytuvatillaiyō? peṇmakaḷ kaimmai eytiya tum avaḷpāluḷḷa iyaṟkai ōṭiviṭukiṟatō? uyir pōy viṭukiṟatō? kaṭavuḷ maṟaintu viṭukiṟārō? paṭaippil eṉṉa kuṟaivu ēṟpaṭukiṟatu? avaḷ etiril varakkūṭātām! kūṭṭaṅ kaḷil pōkak kūṭātām! nalviṉaikaḷil talaikāṭṭak kūṭātām! uṭaiyaiyum uṇavaiyum curukkal vēṇṭumām! appeṇ mūtēviyāy viṭṭāḷām! kaimmai eytiya āṇmakaṉō ellāvaṟṟiṉuṅ kalantu koḷḷalām! avaṉ palliḻanta mutiyaṉāyiṉum patiṉāṟu vayatu kaṭṭaḻakiyai maṇañ ceyyalām! eṉṉa vēṟṟumai! ivvēṟṟumai iyaṟkaiyil uḷḷatā? kaṭavuḷ kaṇṭatā? iḵtu irakkamilā vaṉkaṇāḷar iṭaik kālattil vititta koṭumai! koṭumai!
kuḻantai maṇak koṭumai
nam nāṭṭavariṉ aniyāya meṉṉa? 1ciṟukuḻantai - ōrāṇṭuk kuḻantai - ataṟku maṇameṉṉa teriyum? pacciḷaṅ kuḻavikkup peṇ āṇpāl vēṟṟumaiyātal teriyumā? ak kuḻantaikkut tirumaṇam! atu kaimmai eytiṉāl ataṟkuṅ kaṭṭuppāṭu! akkuḻavi ulakai aṟintatā? illaiyē! atu tāymai eṉṉum cīriya nilaiyai aṭaiyāmalaṉṟō oḻikiṟatu! itu kaṭavuḷ karuttākumā? namatu nāṭṭil ettuṇaip peṇ makkaḷ pālmaṇamaṟā iḷaṅkuḻantaip paruvattil kaimmai eyti varuntukiṟārkaḷ!
jaṉa kaṇitam
1921-ām āṇṭiṉ jaṉak kaṇakkai yoṭṭik kaimmai eytiya peṇmakkaḷ mītu ciṟitu karuttuc celuttuvōm. 
appuḷḷikaḷai uṟṟu nōkkuḻip potuvāka intiyap peṇmaṇikaḷiṉ nilaiyum, ciṟappāka hintu peṇmaṇikaḷiṉ nilaiyum iṉitu pulaṉākum. ikkāla hintu matattaip peṇkolai matam eṉṟu kūṟal mikaiyākātu. intiyā viluḷḷa maṟṟak kaimpeṇkaḷiṉ tokaiyilum, hintu kaimpeṇkaḷ tokaiyē mēmpaṭṭiruttal karutaṟpāṟṟu. intiyāviṉ mottak kaimpeṇ tokai 26,834,838. hintu kaimpeṇ tokai 20,218,780. eñciyuḷḷa tokai mikac ciṟiyatu. atuvum mulimkaḷ - kiṟituvar - pauttar - mutaliyōriluḷḷa kaimpeṇkaḷ aṭaṅkiya tokai.
oruvayatu mutal patiṉaintu vayatu varai, kaimmai eyti yuḷḷa intiyap peṇkuḻantaikaḷ tokai 396,556. itil hintu peṇkuḻantaikaḷ tokai 329,076. ivvaniyāyattai uṟṟu nōkkuṅkaḷ! ivvaḷavu pālmaṇamaṟā iḷaṅkaim peṇkaḷ! eṉṉē! ikkolaikkup poṟuppu yātu? hintu matamaṉṟō? maṟṟa mataṅkaḷiluḷḷavar mīṇṭun tirumaṇañ ceyyum urimai peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. mēlum avar jaṉa kaṇitam eṭutta vēḷaiyil kaimmai eytiyiruntavar. avaruḷ et tuṇaiyō pēr piṉṉai maṇañ ceytiruppar. hintu kuḻantaikaḷiṉ nilaiyō! nilaiyō!.... eṉṟu aḻavēṇṭuvatē!
namatu nāṭṭil ilaṭcakkaṇakkāṉa ciṟukuḻantaikaḷ kaimpeṇkaḷāyirukkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kati eṉṉai? tirumaṇam iṉṉateṉ ṟuṇarāmuṉṉar ēṉ ciṟumiyarait tirumaṇañ ceytu koṭuttal vēṇṭum? kaimmai eyta nērntāl avarai ēṉ kūṭṭil aṭaittu varuttal vēṇṭum? avar vaḻukki vīḻvarēl avarai aṭippatu! ūraiviṭṭu ōṭṭuvatu! camayam nērntāl kolvatu! eṉṉa pāvam! ip pāvaṅkaḷai nikaḻttivarum nāṭṭukku uyvuṇṭō? urimai yuṇṭō? nāyakaṉuṭaṉ orupoḻutēṉum kūṭi iṉpam nukarāta peṇ makaḷ kaimmainilai eytiṉāl, mīṇṭum maṇañ ceytalā kātā? maṇam eṉpatu yātu? pantarā? kōyilā? mantiramā? mālaiyā? orutti oruvaṉuṭaṉ kūṭi iṉpam nukaru nāḷē tirumaṇam muṭinta nāḷākum. iḷamai maṇattaiyum vilakkātu, maṟumaṇattaiyum taṭuppatu aṟamākātu.
maṟumaṇap poṟuppu
uṟṟa vayataṭaintu, pala āṇṭu, talaivaṉuṭaṉ kūṭik kulāvip piḷḷaip pēṟu peṟṟa orutti, kaimpeṇ āvaḷēl, avaḷ maṟumaṇañ ceyyāma lirukkalām. āṉāl avaṭkum taṉ viruppappaṭi naṭakkum urimai iruttal vēṇṭum. avaḷ virumpiṉāl maṟumaṇañ ceytu koḷḷalām. avaḷ vipacāram ceytu keṭṭu aḻivatiṉum, oruvaṉai maṇantu avaṉuṭaṉ vāḻtal ciṟappākum. maṇam virumpuṅ kaim peṇṇait taṭuttaluṅ kūṭātu; virumpātavaḷai valiyuṟut taluṅkūṭātu. poṟuppup peṇṇiṉuṭaiyatā yiruttal vēṇṭum.
cila āṇṭu nāyakaṉuṭaṉ vāḻntu, iṉpam nukarntu, piḷḷaip pēṟu peṟṟuk, kaimmai eytuvōḷ mīṇṭum maṟṟoruvaṉai naccavēṇṭuvatillai. appeṇmaṇi vēṟu pala aṟattuṟaikaḷil iṟaṅki vāḻvu naṭāttal nalaṉ.
cāttiram karmam
pālmaṇamaṟā iḷaṅ kaimpeṇkaḷ uṟṟa vayataṭaintu maṟumaṇañ ceytu koḷvatu aṟamē yākum. ataṟku aṟiñar tuṇai ceytē tīral vēṇṭum. cāttiram - karmam - ivaimītu vīṇpaḻi cumattuvatu aṟiyāmai.
cāttiram etu? iyaṟkai neṟikku māṟupaṭṭu eḻutap paṭṭaṉa vellāñ cāttiraṅkaḷ ākumā? ākā! cāttiram eṉpataṉ poruḷ eṉṉai? itaic cey; itaic ceyyaṟka eṉpatu. iyaṟkaikku uriyataic ceytal vēṇṭum; iyaṟkaikku māṟupaṭṭataic ceytalākātu. itai maṉaccāṉṟu avvap pōtu uṇarttivarum. ituvē cāttiram. tiṉṟu koḻuttup piṟarai aṭakkiyāḷa, iyaṟkaikku muṟṟum araṇ ceyyāta poruḷ potiya, muraṭar cilar eḻutiya ēṭukaḷ yāvuñ cāttiraṅkaḷākā. iyaṟkaiyē periya cāttiram. ataṉ niyatikaḷ, vitikaḷ. avvitikaḷ vaḻi, maṉpatai naṭāttal vēṇṭum. 
kaimmai eytiya peṇ, mīṇṭum maṇam ceyyalākā teṉṟu iyaṟkaiyil ētāyiṉum niyati uṇṭā? peṇ kaimmai eytiyatum avaḷ pāluḷḷa iyaṟkai vēṭkai avaḷai viṭuttu, nīttu ōṭukiṟatā? cāttiram cāttiram eṉṟu iruṭṭaṟaiyil vipacārañ ceytu karuccitaikkalām pōlum! iṅkē,kaṭavuḷ pāvam karmam oṉṟumillai pōlum! veḷippaṭaiyāka oruvaṉai maṇañ ceyvatu maṭṭum pāvam pōlum! eṉṉa aṟiyāmai! iyaṟkaikku māṟupaṭṭa kaṇmūṭi vaḻakkamum kōḻamaiyum nāttikattiṉpāṟ paṭṭaṉavā meṉka.
karmattiṉ mītu ciṟitu karuttuc celuttuvōm. karmam uṇṭu; illai eṉṟu collavillai. teruvē pōkum oru piḷḷaiyiṉ kāl, vaṇṭiyil akappaṭṭu nacuṅkiviṭukiṟatu. atu kaṇṭu, karmam eṉṟu vāḷā kiṭappatu aṟivuṭaimai yākumō? ulakam aṅṅaṉam vāḷā kiṭakkiṟatō? uṭaṉē ak kālukkut takka cikiccai ceyyavē makkaḷ puṟappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. aṟiyāmaiyāl karmam iyalpāka nikaḻum. ataṟkuk kaḻuvāyum iyaṟkaiyilirukkiṟatu. kuḻantai, karmattiṉpaṭi kaimmai eytiyirukkalām. ataṟkuk kaḻuvāy eṉṉai? uṟṟa vayatil atu taṉ iyaṟkai vēṭkaiyait taṇikka maṇañ ceytu koḷvatākum.
karmattiṉ pērālum kaṭavuḷ pērālum namatu nāṭṭil pala koṭumaikaḷ nikaḻttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu iḷam kaimpeṇkaḷait tuṉpuṟuttuvatu. attuṉpam nāṭṭai viṭuttu akalal vēṇṭum. iḷaṅ kaimpeṇkaḷ tuyar kaṇṭu maṉam iraṅkātavar ilar. avartam tuyar kaḷaital peruṅkaṭaṉ eṉṟu niṉaippōrumuḷar. āṉāl niṉaivaic ceyalil koṇara avartam maṉam eḻuvatillai. kāraṇam eṉṉai? nīṇṭanāḷ vaḻakka oḻukkap pēykaḷum, akkam pakkattuk kūkkural - cātip paḻakkam mutaliya pūtaṅkaḷum kuṟuk kiṭutalēyākum. ippēy pūtaṅkaṭkā aṭimaiyāvatu? maṉac cāṉṟeṉuṅ kaṭavuḷ vaḻiyaṉṟō niṟṟal aṟivu? akkaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭiṟku uṇmaiyil uṟutiyum añcā neñcamum tēvai.
kaimpeṇkaḷiṉ tuyar
kaimpeṇkaḷ vīṭukaḷiṟ paṭuntuyarukku ōr aḷavum uṇṭō? cila vēḷaikaḷil avarkaḷai īṉṟa tāy tantai yarum kāykiṉṟaṉar; aṇṇaṉ tampimār veṟukkiṉṟaṉar; maṟṟavarum kuṟai kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. kāyvum veṟuppum poṟātu ettuṇait tāymār taṟkolai ceytukoḷkiṟār; ettuṇaic cakōtarimār vīṭṭai viṭuttu ōṭi alaikiṟār; ettuṇaip pēr evar utaviyum nāṭātu ilai taittum, paḻam viṟṟum, appañ cuṭṭum, kūlivēlai ceytum kāl vayiṟuṇṭu maraṇattai etirnōkki vāḻkiṟār. namatu nāṭṭuk kaimpeṇkaḷ tuyarai eṉṉeṉṟu colvēṉ! evveḻut tāl eḻutuvēṉ! innūlil atupaṟṟi virittuk kūṟalum anāvaciyam.
kaimpeṇkaḷ tuyar kaḷaiya nāṭṭiṟ piṟanta ovvoru varkkum ūkkam eḻuvatāka. ataṟkuriya muṟaikaḷ pala kūṟalām. cila varumāṟu:
(1) 	cāttirak kuppaikaḷaik kaṭṭi aḻalākātu; iyaṟkaic cāttiravaḻi niṟkap payilal vēṇṭum.
(2) 	iḷamai maṇam niṟuttappaṭal vēṇṭum.
(3) 	nāyakaṉuṭaṉ orupoḻutēṉum kūṭi vāḻntu iṉpam nukarātu, kaimmai eytiya peṇ makkaḷ tirumaṇam puriyattakka vaḻiyil vāḻvu muṟaikaḷaic cīrtiruttañ ceytal vēṇṭum.
(4) 	nāyakaṉuṭaṉ kūṭik kulāviya piṉṉaik kaimmai eytuvōrkkup palatiṟa aṟaccālai kalviccālai toḻiṟ cālaikaḷ amaikkappaṭal vēṇṭum.
iḷaṅ kaimpeṇkaḷiṉ ciṟumaiyāl, peṇṇulakiṉ perumaikkuriya tāymaikkum piṟavaṟṟiṟkum peruṅ kēṭu nikaḻkiṟatu. oṉṟum aṟiyāta vayatil tirumaṇameṉṉum peyarāl veṟumaṇam peṟṟup piṉṉaik kaimpeṇṇāyk kiṭappatā peṇpiṟaviyiṉ nōkkam? peṇ aṭaiyattakka perum pēṟṟukkuk kaimmainilai peruntaṭaiyākavum niṟkiṟatu. kaimpeṇkaḷāl āṇulakiṟkum pala tiṟak kēṭukaḷ uṇṭu. ātalāl kaimmaip peṇkaḷum peṇṇulakil cērntavarkaḷākalāṉ, avarkaḷum taṅkaḷiṉattavarukkuriya viḻuppam peṟutaṟku evarum iṭaiyūṟu ceyvōrāka niṟṟalākātu; iṭaiyūṟāka niṟṟal tāykkolai ceyta pāvamāka muṭiyum. kaimpeṇkaḷiṉ ciṟumai eṉṉum nōy nīkkam, peṇṇulakiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattukkup perum vaḻi tiṟakkum. peṇṇulakiluḷḷa akkuṟai nīṅka, ellām valla āṇṭavaṉ nāṭṭavarkku nallaṟivu koḷuttuvāṉāka.
tirumaṇañ ceytu kaṉṉi vāḻvu naṭāttum peṇ taṉ kaṇavaṉai iḻakkap peṟuvaḷēl, mīṇṭum maṟumaṇam purintu koḷḷalām.
vākkuṟuti koṭutta piṉṉarō - nīrvārttut tattañ ceyta piṉṉarō - meyyuṟal nikaḻā muṉṉarō - koḻunaṉ ivvulaka vāḻvu nīppaṉēl, peṇ maṟumaṇañ ceytu koḷḷalām.
uṟuti moḻiyum mantira moḻiyum oruttiyiṉ piramacariya viratattaik kulaippaṉavākā. avaḷ maṟṟavaṉukkuk koṭukkappaṭalām.	-vaciṣṭar
“Hinduism is the only religion which presents the sad anomalies of thousands of child widows”	-Ganga Ram


13. peṇmai - tāymai  - iṟaimai
(peṇmai - tāymai - iṟaimai - aṉpē iṟai - tāymaiyil iṟaimai ilaṅkum vitam - peṇ teyvam - peṇṇiṉ iṟaimai - srīrāmapirāṉ muyaṟci - pāratap pōr - ellām peṇṇāl - ellām peṇṇil.)
cemmainalam aṟiyāta citaṭaroṭun tirivēṉai
mummaimalam aṟuvittu mutalāya mutalvaṉṟāṉ
nammaiyumōr poruḷākki nāycivikai ēṟṟuvitta
ammaiyeṉak karuḷiyavā ṟārpeṟuvā raccōvē
	-vātavūraṭikaḷ
peṇmai  eṉpatu anātiyāka muḻu mutaṟporuḷiṉ orukūṟāyiruppatu. appeṇmai taṇmaic cattiyāyt taṉak kuriya kaṭaṉai āṟṟi varukiṟatu. accattiyiṉ kūṟē peṇṇula keṉpatu. peṇ, makaḷākap piṟantu, piṉṉai maṉaiviyāki, piṉṉait tāyāki, muṭivil iṟaiyākiṟāḷ. peṇṇi ṉiṭattu ittuṇai nilaikaḷ iruttal innūṟkaṇ viḷakkap paṭṭirukkiṟatu.
peṇmai
peṇmai eṉpatu kaṭpulaṉāyatōr amaitittaṉmai. amaiti nilaiyē ciṟanta kaṭavuḷnilai eṉṟu maṟai muḻaṅkukiṟatu. kaṭavuḷai yāṇṭum cāntam cāntam eṉṟu vētāntam pōṟṟukiṟatu. amaiti, peṇ vaṭivākak kaṇṇukkuk kāṭci aḷikkiṟatu; kāṭci aḷippatōṭu nillātu, ulaka vāḻvil talaippaṭṭuk kaṭavuḷ neṟiyaik kāṭṭukiṟatu; anneṟiyāl iṟaimaik kāṭciyaiyum vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. peṇmaikkaṇuḷḷa innuṭpaṅkaḷai yellām ulakam uṇarvatāka.
tāymai
peṇmaikkuḷḷa perumai yātu? peṇmaiyiṉ māṭṭu, ulaka vaḷarccik kuriyatum toṇṭuk kuriyatumākiya tāymai politalāṉ, atu perumaiyuṭaiyatākiṟatu. peṇmaik kuḷḷa perumai yellām tāymaiyāl eṉka.
tāymaiyil nilavuvatu iṟaimai. iṟaimai peṇmai yiṉ muṭintanilaiyākum.
iṟaimai
iṟai etu? camaya nūlkaḷ palavāṟu kūṟum. akkūṟṟuk kaḷai īṇṭu ārāya vēṇṭuvatillai. poy, poṟāmai, avā, cīṟṟam, taṉṉalam mutaliyavaṟṟaik kaṭanta oṉṟu iṟai eṉpatu. oruvar uḷḷattil aṉpu nikaḻum pōtu ippoy, poṟāmai mutaliyaṉa nilavumō? poy, poṟāmai, avā mutaliyavaṟṟāl eriyum oruvaṉ uḷḷattil aṉpu otuṅki niṟṟal, (ovvoruvar tam tam) anupavattāl uṇarak kūṭiyatu.
aṉpu
aṉpu eṉpatu poy poṟāmai mutaliyavaṟṟaik kaṭantu niṟpatu. poy, poṟāmai mutaliyavaṟṟaik kaṭantu niṟkum oṉṟē iṟai eṉṟuñ collappaṭukiṟatu. ākavē iṟaiyē aṉpu, aṉpē iṟaiyātal kāṇka.
aṉpuñ civamum iraṇṭeṉpar aṟivilār
aṉpē civamāva tārum aṟikilār
aṉpē civamāva tārum aṟintapiṉ
aṉpē civamā yamarntirun tārē 	- tirumūlar
tāymaiyil iṟaimai ilaṅkum vitam
peṇ, piḷḷai peṟṟatun tāyākiṟāḷ. tāy piḷḷaiyai vaḷarkkap pukuṅkāl, avaḷ uḷḷattil iṟai maikkuriya nīrmaikaḷellām patikiṉṟaṉa. toṇṭu, tiyākam, taṉṉala maṟuppu mutaliyaṉa avaḷ māṭṭu arumpukiṉṟaṉa. tāy kaim māṟu karutik kuḻantaikkut toṇṭu ceyvatillai. taṉakkuḷḷa ellāvaṟṟaiyum - camayam nēriṉ uyiraiyum - piḷḷai nalattukkuk koṭukkat tāy viraintu niṟkiṟāḷ. taṉṉalaṅ karutik kuḻantaiyai varuttun tāy yāṇṭum irāḷ. payaṉ karutāt toṇṭu, tiyākam, taṉṉala maṟuppu mutaliyaṉa cērnta oṉṟē tāymai eṉka. innīrmaikaḷ uḷḷa iṭattil poṟāmai avā mutaliyaṉa irumpuṇṭa nīrpōla oṭuṅkip pōkum. innilaipeṟṟa tāyiṉ uḷḷattil eṉṉa nilavum? aṉpākiya iṟaiyaṉṟō nilavum? tāyuḷḷattil aṉpē ūrntiruttalāl, aṉpukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭākat tāyaṉpaik koḷvatu āṉṟōr vaḻakkam. tāy eṉiṉum aṉpeṉiṉum okkum.
iṉṉum iraṇṭoru piḷḷaikaḷaip peṟun tāyaṉpu colaṟpālataṉṟu. avvaṉpu kūṭap peṟṟa tāy, maṟṟap piḷḷaikaḷaiyum taṉ piḷḷaikaḷaippōṟ karutum orunilai eytukiṟāḷ. annilai evvuyiraiyum taṉṉuyirpōl pārkkum perunilai kūṭṭum. apperunilaikkut tāymai nilaik kaḷāṉā  yirukkiṟatu. poy poṟāmai kaṭanta aṉpē, tāymai yākaliṉ, tāymai iṟaimaiyākiṟatu.
aṉpukkaṭavuḷ kōyil
poy poṟāmaikaḷait tavirkkum toṇṭu, tiyākam, taṉṉala miṉmai mutaliyaṉa paṭiyum uḷḷamē aṉpuk kaṭavuḷuk kuriya kōyil. aṉpuk kaṭavuḷ, nilaipēṟākat tāyākiya peṇ makaḷiṭattil oḷirkiṟatu. peṇmaiyil tāymaiyum, tāymai yil iṟaimaiyum polivatu karutattakkatu.
peṇ teyvam
peṇmai eṉpatu amaitit taṉmai eṉṟu mēlē collappaṭṭatu. amaitinilai iṟaimai eṉpatu. iṟaimai yuṭaiya tāyai veṟum peṇṇeṉṟā kūṟuvatu? avaḷai peṇ teyvameṉṟu pōṟṟutal vēṇṭum. peṇ - teyvam! peṇ - teyvam! peṇ - teyvam!
muḻu mutaṟ poruḷ, taṇmai vemmai yeṉum iraṇṭu caktiyāka ulakai iyakkukiṟateṉpatu nūl toṭakkattil kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. at taṇmaiyiṉ kūṟu peṇṇulaku. peṇ piṟantu, ulaka vaḷarccikkut tuṇaipurintu, mīṇṭum at taṇmait teyvamākiṟāḷ.
peṇ, makaḷākat tōṉṟiṉāḷ; maṉaiviyāka vāḻn tāḷ; tāyākat toṇṭu ceytāḷ. ippoḻutu teyva mākak kāṭci aḷikkiṟāḷ. ulakīr! akkāṭci kāṇmiṉ; teyvam teyvam eṉṟu eṅku ōṭukiṟīr? nūṟkaḷai ēṉ ārāy kiṟīr? itō teyvam; peṇ teyvam; kāṇuṅkaḷ; kaṇṭu vaḻipaṭuṅkaḷ.
peṇmai eṉpatu, eṉputōl pōrtta uṭalaṉṟu. avvuṭaliṉuḷḷē yuḷḷa nuṇmai,  iṟaimai, peṇmai eṉka. attakaiya peṇmai vāḻka.
āṇmakkaḷē! uṅkaḷ perumai peṇṇuṭaṉ porunti niṟpatu. uṅkaḷaip peṟṟavaḷ peṇ; uṅkaḷai vaḷarppavaḷ peṇ; uṅkaḷōṭu vāḻpavaḷ peṇ; uṅkaḷaip peṇṇākku pavaḷ peṇ. nīṅkaḷ peṇṇākātu, atāvatu peṇmainilai peṟātu, atāvatu amaiti nilai eytātu, viṭutalaiyaṭaital iyalātu. peṇṇāl nalaṉaṭaiyum nīṅkaḷ appeṇṇu lakaiyā veṟuppatu? tuṟappatu? naṉṟi kollātēyuṅkaḷ; tāynaṉṟi kollātēyuṅkaḷ; ennaṉṟi koṉṟārkkum uyvuṇṭām uyvillai- ceynnaṉṟi koṉṟa makaṟku eṉṉum poyyāmoḻiyai nōkkuṅkaḷ.
peṇṇiṟku matippuk koṭuṅkaḷ; urimai koṭuṅkaḷ; vaṇakkam celuttuṅkaḷ. peṇṇai matittup pōṟṟalē nākarikam. avaḷaik kaṭṭuppaṭuttal - aṭimaippaṭuttal - koṭumaiyāka naṭattal - anākarikam.
peṇṇalaṉ uṅkaḷ nalaṉ; uṅkaḷ nāṭṭu nalaṉ. peṇ kaṇṇīrviṭum nāṭu vaṟumai nāṭākum; aṭimai nāṭākum. aḵtu erintu pōkum; karintu pōkum. oru peṇṇukku srīrāmapirāṉ evvaḷavu muyaṉṟār; ettuṇai iṭukkaṇuṟṟār; ettuṇaippōr purintār! oru peṇṇiṉ poruṭṭup pāratap perumpōr naṭantatu! peṇṇiṉ tuyar tīrppatu āṇmakaṉ kaṭamai. avaḷatu tuyar tīrkka iyalātavaṉ āṇ makaṉallaṉ. peṇṇiṉ ciṟumai pōkka uyiraiyun tuṟakkalām. avaḷatu perumai kākkavē āṇmakaṉ piṟantirukkiṟāṉ. peṇṇillaiyēl ulakētu? uyirētu? aṉpētu? kaṭavuḷētu? ellām peṇṇāl; ellām peṇṇil. attakaiya peṇṇai - tāyai - teyvattaip pōṟṟuṅkaḷ; pōṟṟuṅkaḷ.
eṅkaṇum peṇmai polika; eṅkaṇun tāymai ōṅka; eṅkaṇum iṟaimai vāḻka. vantēmātaram.
oppuṭaiya mātarum oṇporuḷum nī 	- appar
tāyē yāki vaḷarttaṉai pōṟṟi 	- māṇikkavācakar
tāymaip peṇṇiṉam vāḻntu taḻaikka
	tāymaip peṇṇuyir tantiṭal kāṭci
tāymaip peṇṇeṉac cāṉṟukaḷ vēṇṭā
	tāymaip peṇṇiṉam veṉṟu taḻaikka.	(1)
ulaka mellām utippiṭan tāymai
	utittut taṅki oḻukiṭan tāymai
kalaiyiṉ mūlaṅ kamaḻiṭan tāymai
	kaṭavu ḷaṉpu curappiṭan tāymai.	(2)
vāṉai nōkka vayaṅkiṭun tāymai
	maṇṇai nōkka viḷaṅkiṭun tāymai
kāṉam māmalai kāṭṭiṭum tāymai
	kaṭalum āṟum karuṇaiyiṉ tāymai. 	(3)
karuṇaip peṇṇaik kaṭivatō āṇmai
	kaṭivō rentak karuvaḻi vantār?
aruḷai aṟṟavar ārtta moḻikaḷ
	aṉalaik kakkum arunara kākum.	(4)
peṇṇaip pēyeṉap pēcutal naṉṟō
	periya māyaiyeṉ ṟēcutal naṉṟō
taṇmai yillā vaṉattu vilaṅkum
	taṭavip peṇṇalam tāṅkutal kāṇmiṉ.	(5)
pāvai tāli aṟuttiṭal mūrkkam
	paṭṭam muṇṭai eṉ ṟītalum mūrkkam
ētu maṟṟa iḻivukaḷ mūrkkam
	ellām vīḻtta iḷaiñar eḻumiṉ.	(6)
āṇum peṇṇum camaṉcamaṉ aiyō
      āṇiṉ kōlmika aṉṉai viḻuntāḷ
kōṇal nīkkiṭuṅ kātal maṇamē
        kōtil mārkkam kulavum iṉitē	(8)

1	God is unknown and unknowable.	-Herbert  Spencer
1	yāṉ eḻutiyuḷḷa tirukkuṟaḷ virivuraiyil poṟivāyil aintavittāṉ, aintavittāṉ āṟṟal mutaliya kuṟaṭpākkaṭku eṉṉāl poṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷai nōkkuka. pulaṉaṭakka nuṭpattai murukaṉ allatu aḻaku mutaliya nūlkaḷilum eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ.
1	iyalpuṭaiya mūvar eṉpataṟkut tirukkuṟaḷ virivuraiyil yāṉ eḻutiyuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷaip pārkka.
1.	* * * They must understand that the life - giving fluid called the semen, which is produced in the creative organs of the man, is of great value in the upbuilding of his own body. It is only within comparatively recent times that the marvellous power of the creative fluid in building up and making over the body of the individual has been thoroughly understood. In sexual intimacies there is a discharge of this creative fluid from the body of the man, but where there is a full response on the part of the wife, there seems to be an exchange of magnetism or energy which makes up for the loss. If however, his desire alone is active and she is simply fulfilling a supposed wifely duty, she gives nothing to him, and he therefore, suffers a definite loss in vitality. It is claimed by some that such one-sided intimacies are almost as harmful to the man as masturbation. Frequent indulgence upon this basis must result in a loss of vital energy which will deprive the man of the strength and vigor needed for the performance of his life takes * * *
 - B –Macfadden
